|| śrīḥ ||
āpastambakalpasatraparicayaḥ pumarthān svasvāhanadhigamayituṃ dharmaniyatān śrutismṛtyācāraiḥ samayaniyataiḥ svapravitataiḥ| matuiṃ śaktyutsāha dhṛtimavicalāṃ ca pradiśatu prajānāṃ rājñaścāniśamapi sa niṣkāraṇo dharmaḥ ṣaḍaṅgo vedo'dhyeyo jñeya ‘' iti śrutiḥ sānaṃ vedādhyayanamantareṇa viprasya svarūpalābhamevā sambhāvanīyaṃ adhyayanena āmuṣmikaṃ śreyassusādhaṃ ca manyate| tasmātsāṅgamadhītyaiva dharmeṣu , "niyameṣu tapaśśabdaḥ, tadaṃtikrame vidyākarma nissravati brahma sahāpatyādetasmāt | kartapatyamanāyuṣyaṃ ca | tasmādṛṣayo'vareṣu na jāyante niyamātikramāt| śrutarṣayaśca sambha vanti kecit pracāramāsedivāṃ- sīti bhagavataḥ pāṇinervacasā pratīmaḥ| yataḥ ‘tena proktam 'iti pravacanārthakapadena prakramya tittiriprabhṛtīn vedapravakṛniva kalpa pravahnapi ‘purāṇaprokteṣu brāhmaṇakalpeṣu ' iti smarati | yadyapi ‘purāṇāni kalpān' ityannāyamāna iva kalpaśabdastre 'pi prayogadarśakavedabhāgapara iti tasya ca pravacamamaiṣyāmnāya iva svārasikamiti ca vakṭuṃ śakyate , tathā'pi ‘pārāśaryaśilālibhyāṃ bhikṣunaṭasūtrayoḥ ' iti sūtre kalpasūtranibandhānuvādino mīmāṃsā darśanapravartakasya jaiminerācāryaṃ bādarāyaṇaṃ nirdiśan kalpasūtra pravacanaparamparāyāḥ svaprācīnatāṃ bāḍhamevānumanyate pāṇiniḥ| kāśyapakauśikagrahaṇaṃ kalpe niyamārtham ' iti vadan vārti- kakārakātyāyanaśca kaṇṭhata evāha tatpracīnatām | kalpasūtranibandheṣu ca āpastambīyo'pyanyatamaḥ prathate| niba dho'yaṃ pāṇineratiprācīna iti niścite bhāṣyakṛtsammatiṃ darśa yāmaḥ ; –‘tadasyāstyasminnitimatup ' iti sūtre ‘sanmātre ca ṛṣidarśanāt' iti vārtikam | tatra ca bhāṣyam ; sanmātre ca śraṛṣirdarśayati matupam ‘yavamatībhiryupaṃ prokṣatīti' iti | idaṃ ca vākyam āpastambakalpe saptamaprabhaṃ navamakhaṇḍe navamasūtre ‘agreṇā vaṭaṃ prave yūpaṃ nidhāya' ityārabhya ‘yūpaṃ prakṣālyāthainaṃ yavamatībhiḥ 1 jaimineḥ kalpanibandhānuvādi cāne vyaktaṃ bhaviṣyati | vi prokṣati' iti dṛśyate | enamityasya vivaraṇatayā yūpaśabdaḥ prayu- jyate patañjalinā || ko'trāpastambaḥ kadā'sau ? patañjalinā kalpasūtravākyānāmṛṣivākyatvokteḥ pāṇinināpi purātanaprokatayā kalpasūtrāṇāmanuvādāt ṛṣikulasya kalyādi 450 varṣāntatāyāssiddhatvāt ṛṣipraṇītānāṃ kalpasūtrāṇāṃ kalyā- ditaḥ 450 varṣāntā prācīnatā dṛḍhaṃ niścīyata iti tu bhagavaddattaḥ | yājñavalkyaśca - - yamāpastambasaṃvartāḥ kātyāyana bṛhaspatī | parāśaravyāsaśaṅkhalikhitā dakṣagautamau || śātātapo vasiṣṭhaśca dharmaśāstraprayojakāḥ || iti svapravartanīye dharmanibandhe prācīnadharmanibandhapravartakān smaran teṣvanyatamaṃ kalayatyāpastambam | atrāha bodhāyanakalpavyākhyātā yajñeśvara:- medhāṃ bodhāyano vo vinaratu sahitappaṣṭisāhasraśiṣyaiḥ yasyāpastamba ekādhikaśatayajuṣāṃ pārago mukhyaśiṣyaḥ | sūtraṃ prāyuta naikakramavidhinipuṇādhvaryunānāprayoga- vyaktiśānopayuktasphuṭamṛdugahanaprauḍhasandarbhagarbham || kalpāmṛtaṃ kṣitisuraprakarāya yo'dāt vedāmburāśimadhimathya nijaprabhāvāt | bodhāyanāya munivanditapādapadma- dvandvāya kaṇvatanayāya namo'stu tasmai | iti | prāyaścittavicintananāmni nibandhe ca- bodhāyanaṃ jaiminiṃ ca kauṣītakyāśvalāyanau | vādhūlakāpastambādīn naumi śākhāpravartakān || iti śākhāpravartakeṣvāpastambagaṇanāṃ darśayati | bahucaśāṭyāyani- • paiṅgichandogādi padapuraskāreṇa brāhmaṇānāṃ pramāṇatāṃ pradarśayan bhagavānāpastambaḥ śākhāvibhājakādbhagavato vedavyāsādanantarakā lika ityapyadhyavasātumastyavasaraḥ | " V11 parāśareṇa saha dharmaśāstrapravartakeṣu parigaṇanaṃ syāsapūrvakāli tāmeva sudughaṭayati | tattatapasvijanasukṛtādhīnaparamapuruṣānugrahanivandhanastatam prādurbhavo'pi ‘ viśvāmitrasya sūkkaṃ bhavatyetena vai, ityādiṣu ‘brahma svayambhuvabhyātarṣat tadṛṣīṇāmṛṣitvam' ityādiṣu ca kṣutibhirevā vedyate| eṣa eva ca nyāyo brāhmaṇeṣvapi | ta pracāraḥ | tadudāharaṇamapi tadanurodhyeveti nāvaśyaṃ śākhāvibhāgotta rabhāvitaiveti niścayasaṃbhava , nanu tathāspi vistṛtadaśaśimadhyasaṃgṛhītāṃśacatustohitākara ṇena pratyekaṃ śiṣyapravartakādbhagavato vyāsādanantaraṃ vaiśampāyanaśiṣya paramparāpravacanaviṣayaśāskhābhedeṣu tatra taittirīyā nāma dvibhedā bhavanti aukhaḥ khāṇḍikeyāśceti| tatra khāṇḍikeyā nāma pañcabhedā bhavanti kāletā hairaṇyakeśī bhāra dvājī āpastambī ceti' iti caraṇavyūhe ddhitīyakhaṇḍe āpastamba pravartitaśākhāyāstannāmnā vyavahāramūlaprasiddhikīrtanāt śākhā cāryarūpa eva bhagavanāpastambaḥ śakyate | bādarāyaṇaśiṣyaparamparāghaṭaka eva bhagavānāpastambaḥ kalpa sūtrakṛdapītyapi tadīyakalpastreṣu " iti vājasaneyinassamāmanani eke, kajhati brāhmaṇas, kāṭhakāḥ, kālabavibrāhmaṇasa, kauṣītakinaḥ chandogabrāhmaṇam , tāṇḍakam , tāṇḍinaḥ bahvabrāhmaṇam bhāṭavikam vājasaneyakam , śāṭyāyanakam , tattaduṣṭabhāgaparatayā pyupateḥ vakṣyamāṇopajīvanakrameṇaprācīnatāsiddheśca pravaktaparamparā parigaṇitaśca bhagavānanya evāpastamba ityava dheyam | 1 śulbapraśnaṃ karavindadhipena-takṣaśāne gāgryāgastyādibhira alisaṃkhyayoktaṃ rathaparimāṇaślokamudāharati iti sūtrāvatāraṇadī- nāt gāgryāgastyādyuttarakāliko'yamāpastamba ityapyavagamyate | kalpabhājyapravati". sānukarūpameva praṇītānyapi bhāṣyādipraṇayanena samujjīvyamānāni svārthabodhanakṣamāṇi jāgrati || tatrāpastambakalpasūtravṛndasya dhūrtasvāmikapardisvaminau bhāṣya kārau rāmānicit vṛttikāraśca parirakṣakatāyāṃ saṃpratipadyate || yāśca pratardehavikāratām. ityevaṃ svīye kalpe vedārthanirṇaya parikaraṃ nirūpayati jaiminiḥ tameva ca satyāṣāḍhaḥ | evamanādeḥ kālātvahan anuṣṭhānatatpramāṇa tatpracāra prakā rāṇāṃ vicāraḥ taistaiḥ kalpakāraiḥ saṃkṣepavistarābhyāṃ yathāyathaṃ nirū pyate iti bahuṣu kalpanibandheṣu ko vā yajñakarmāpekṣito nyāyanirṇa yādiranirūpito'vaśiṣyate iti mahāprājñamunijana pūrvapūrvakṣuṇṇe naiva pathā jaiminirapyācāryaḥ saṃcaramāṇa bahusaṃgrāhiṇaṃ vipulaṃ darśane pravartayāmāseti viśvāsimaḥ| jaiministre tasahaṣināmagrahaṇamapye tadupapādayati | X evaṃ bādarāyaṇo'pi—‘pāriplavāthaṃ iti cenna viśeṣitatvāt iti sūtre pāripyavaśabdena (20 pra 6 khe 7 sūtre) āpastambena yāni dakṣiṇenāhavanīyaṃ hotā hiraṇyakaśipāvupaviśati pāriplavaṃ bhauva nyavaṃ cācikhyāsan' ityanenānūditāni , āśvalāyanena ca ,-- dakṣiṇata āhavanīyasya hiraṇyakaśipāvāsīno'bhiṣiktāya puṣā mātyaparivṛtāyagrāśca pariplavamācakṣīta' ityādinā navāhasamāpa nīyāni yānyākhyānaśaṃsanāni vihitāni tānyevānuvadatīti tato's rvācīnameva bādarāyaṇīyaṃ darśanamapi| gṛhyatātparyadarśane'pi – nānāsādhanakāḥ nānāśākhāsthāṅgakāḥ mīmāṃsānyāyasahasra- nirdhāryavacanavyaktikāḥ mandabuddhibhiridānīntanairdūrśānāḥajñāne cānuṣṭhātumaśaktāḥ kathaṃ cana pratyaveyuriti kṛpāviṣṭacetaskatayā sūtrakāreṇa ‘yaśaṃ vyākhyāsyāma ' iti paribhāṣāyāmekaviṃśatiyaśān saṃkṣepato vyākhyāya tāvanmātreṇānupayogāt ‘athāto darśapūrṇa māsa' ityārabhya śrotā haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ kṣāmavatyādayo naimi sikāḥ prasaṅgātkāmyāśca viśeṣato vyākhyātāḥ ityāha sudarśanā | atra ca sarvavidhamīmāṃsānyāyavṛndamapi pūrvapūrvakalpasūtrapravartaka- muniparamparayā saṃkṣepavistarābhyāṃ pravaryamānaṃ pracaratītyabhiprāyaḥ | sati caivaṃ yajñaśātravyākhyā tatparigṛhītavedavyākhyā- nibandhā iva mīmāṃsādarśanamapi yajñaśātravyākhyārūpameveti siddha bhavati | itthaṃ yathāśāstravyākhyābhirakhilābhiraikakaṇṭhyenaiva vedārtheti ca kartavyatā pūraṇīyeti siddham | yajñaśātravyākhyā tatparigṛhītavedavyākhyā tathā vidhamīmāṃ sādarśanarahasya vedina eva yajñaśātrabhūtakalpasūavivaraṇe prabhavanti tathābhūtā eva dhūrtasvāmi kapardisvāmi rāmāṇḍārā āpa ' tasbīyakalpasūtranibandhe bhāṣyakāravṛttikārāḥ yajñānuṣṭānamārga saṃrakṣakavipulanibandhapraṇetāraḥ prathantetarām | XIV 11 11 sāyamupakramaṃ prātarapavargamagnihotraṃ sūtrakārāṇāṃ matam ; sāyamādikāmo dravyaniyamaśca | mīmāṃsakānāṃ tu karmanāmaivāgnihotramiti na samudāyasya , teṣāṃ yasmin kasmin home ārambhaḥ | (bhā.) āgrayaṇena yakṣye svarga lokamavāptavānīti saṅkalpa ādau mīmāṃsakamatyā ; yatrānānātaḥ kāmaḥ na tatra kāmayitavyamityupadeśa| (bhā). āgantukatvādāgrayaṇasya aviruddha ajyānyaḥ kriyante | mīmāṃsakānāmāśrayaṇasya pradhānya mityanityasya prayoga iti saguṇaviguṇa- sādhyā prakṛtiriti tatra tasya sarcatantram | 1 (bhā). 11 yadyapi mīmāṃsakaiḥ vaiśyadeyāmikṣāsāhacaryāt tṛtīyapraśna-piṣṭalepaphalīkaraṇahomaviṣare - asya homasya ādityo devatā hṛdayāntarapuruṣo vā iti vṛttikṛt rāmāṇḍāraḥ turīyapraśna-vanaspatījyābhirārādhanaṃ kariṣyāmīti iti , āhutibhirārādhitatvāt iti ca bhāṣyakṛt dhūrta svāmī turīyapraśna–jyotiṣmatīgthā yasyāgnihotrārthamuddhato'gnirahu te'gnihotre'nugacchati tasyotpattiḥ (bhā) ya pūrvamanugata āhavanīyo'gniḥ sa evāyamani yo'tra devatābhūto'gniḥ | āśīrbhirupasthānena yācate , devatā kopaprasaṅgāt iti vṛttikṛt | ṣaṣṭhe prakṣe-~navānāmagraprāpa rṇaṃ devānām , tadāgrayaṇakarma iti | svāmibhāṣyam | saptame praśne-vaiśvadevaśabdanirvacane-atredaṃ rānumeya vedāvagamyāni karmāṇi ata eva tebhya prathamamanuṣṭheyebhyo'pi pūrva śrautānāṃ vyākhyānaṃ kṛtam iti | pratyakṣacativihiteṣu jijñāsāyāḥ prathamabhāvitvāt anumita vedārthajijñāsāyāścaramabhāvitvāt iti ca | ‘prokṣaṇīsaṃskāraḥ pātraprokṣa iti darśapūrṇamāsavattūṣṇīm 'iti gṛhyasūtramapyenamarthamabhipraiti, na hi sūtrakāro muniranirūpitapūrvamartha- matideśyaṃ manyate | sūtrapāṭhasampradāyaḥ āpastambakalpasūtreṣu saṃhitārūpeṇaiva pāṭhaḥ khaṇḍikārūpā eva tvavāntara vibhāgāḥ katipaya khaṇḍikābhiśca paṭalābhidhānā vibhāgāḥ paṭalaiśca katipayaiḥ praśnāḥ iti sthitirdṛśyate prāyo likhita sūtrakośeṣu | vedbhāṣyakṛtassāyaṇācāryā api tatratatra anuvākārambhādiṣu atra kalpa. ityupakramya vākyānyudāharanti, na ca tāni khaṇḍātma kāni vā sūtrātmakāni vā dṛśyante, kiṃtu tattadapekṣitavākyasamu dāyātmakānyeva | rudradattavṛttyā saha kyāleḍnāmnā hūṇena mudrā pite jarmanabhāṣārūpeṇānūdite tu saṃpuṭatrayaṃ vibhaktasūtrakrama caturviṃśati- praśnaghaṭitameva vilasati | vājapeyādibhāge kapardibhāṣye'pi prāya- sūtraparigraheṇa vivaraṇaṃ dṛśyate | anena ca kalpasūtreṣveṣu sūtra- bibhāgena pāṭho'pi sāmpradāyika ityavagantuṃ śakyate | XXXII itthaṃ ca saṃhitārūpeṇaivādhyāpanena pracāryamāṇaṃ sūtravṛndaṃ sāṃpra- dāyikārthānusandhānasaraṇyā klṛptavibhāgayaivānupūrvyā bhāṣyakāra- prabhṛtayo'nusamadadhuḥ | sa ca paryapālyataiva yathāyathaṃ vyākhyātṛbhiḥ yathā rudradattena iti siddham | - ayaṃ cāṃśaḥ bhāṣyavṛttikāravacobhiraṣyavagamyate, tathāhi - iti sūtracchedaḥ | pādam' ityantamekaṃ sūtram | ubhayatra sūtre mantrayorvirodhaparihārārtha bhāṣyam | brahmaprakaraṇoktasya kālavidhānametatsūtram | dīpyamānaṃ parāpaśyettata āhṛtyāgni kalpayedityekaṃ sūtram | nahīna manvāhareyuriti sūtramarthasaṃgatyā utkṛṣyavyākhyāyate | sūtrakrameṇa mantrāṇāṃ sūtrakārapāṭhasya vivakṣitatvāt | tathāgnirādheya ityekaṃ sūtraṃ na bhavati | tau na paśau karoti ityanena sūtreṇa pratiṣedhaḥ | na some iti sūtrāntareṇa | hṛdayama bhighārayatītyantamekaṃ sūtram | iti bhāṣyakāravṛttikāravacanāni tatra tatra sūtrabhāganirṇāyakatayā pravṛttāni tatra matibhedasaṃbhavaṃ tannirāsaṃ pāṭhasampradāyasya sūkṣmekṣikā grāhyatāṃ tanmūlabhūtaṃ tadāmnāna sampradāyabhedaṃ ca grāhayanti | satyapi satsampradāyaparipālanaśradvāpauṣkalye sūtrakārabhāvapari- jñānasaṃpradāyaparirakṣaṇaṃ kacit kenāpi kāraṇena dauṣkaryagrastamapi bhavitumarhatīti kapardisvāmibhāṣyādavagamyate | yato hi —— bhuvo- kthyena rathantarasāmnā bhūtikāmo yajeta' ityasmin (52-6-26) sūtre ' na vidyate tāṇḍināmiti na śakyate vyākhyātuma iti bhāgya grantho dṛśyate | āpastambakalpabhāṣyabhāgavyavasthā. āpastambakalpasūtraṃ dhartakhāmi kapardisvāmibhyāṃ sāmpradāyika- gūḍhabhāvāviṣkaraṇaparabhāṣyabhāgābhyāṃ bhūṣitaṃ virājata ityadhūma |

Xxx111 tatra kati praśnā dhūrtasvāminā kati ca kapardisvāminā vyākhyātā iti viśayaṃ paryahārṣīt traividyavṛddhaḥ prayogadīpikākṛt tālavṛtta- nivāsī | tena hi, 'āpastambīyasūtrasya dhūrtasvāmivyākhyānusāreṇa śāstrāntara- siddhānapekṣitānupasaṃhṛtya agnicayanaparyantānāṃ karmaṇāṃ prayogavṛttiḥ kriyate' iti svagranthārambhe; 6 kapadyebhiprāyeṇa 'vājapeyāḍhi viśvasṛjāmayanaparyantānāṃ karmaṇāṃ prayoga ucyate iti svagranthamadhye vājapeyārambhe pratijñāyate | anena ca ādito dhūrtasvāminā vājapeyamārabhya kapardisvāminā ca bhāṣyaṃ prāṇāyīti sūcitaṃ bhavati | , " ubhābhyāṃ pṛthageva bhāṣyaṃ samagrameva racitamityapi kuto na kalpyatāmiti codyaṃ tu granthopalambhamātranirastamapi taṭasthaprācīna- prāmāṇika nibandhṛvacassaṃvādena nirasyāmaḥ | śrīmannigamāntadeśika- viracitāyāḥ śrībhāṣyasārasaṅgraharūpāyā adhikaraṇasārāvalyāḥ 11 śaślokavivaraṇe tattanayaiḥ varadanāthācāryaiḥ karmakāṇḍe jaiminiḥ devatākāṇḍe kāśakṛtsnaḥ śārīrake bādarāyaṇa iti kartṛbhedācchāstra- medamāśaṅkaya 'na hyekasya kāryasya eka eva karteti niyamo'sti ! dṛśyate hi pūrvottarayovṛttyoḥ ekanibandhatvamāpastambīyassūtra- vyākhyānayordhūrtasvāmika pārdesvāminibandhanayorapi ' ityuktam || vasanta- evam aṣṭamaprane prathamapaṭalānte yo vai vasanto'bhūt ' ityādi- sūtre 'punarvasantādiniyamāt' ityādibhāṣyamupādāya śabdārthavicāraṃ prakramya 'atra kapardisvāminā 'śiśire dīkṣante vasanta uttiṣṭhante' iti sūtre ' dvādaśaprathamarāśyoryadā sūryodaya- stadā vasanta ityuktatvāt " iti rāmāṇḍāravṛtterdarśanāt (21 - 2 - 8) sūtrasthaṃ kapardibhāpyaṃ pramāṇayatā rāmāṇḍāreṇa tatra dhūrtasvāminā bhāṣyāpraṇayanaṃ sūcyate | na hi sadapi tatra dhūrtasvāmibhāṣyaṃ nālaṃ vivakṣitārthasādhanāyeti nipuṇataramavaśyāpekṣitākhilagūḍhārthanirū- SROUTHA VOL. I. paṇaparasya dhūrtasvāmino bhāṣyakṛto nyūnatāṃ śaṅkitumarhati kaścinni paka' | XXXIV nāpi bhāṣyabhāgadvayaikakaṇṭhyaṃ pṛthaksamagrabhāṣyāpraṇayanaṃ tra ma nvānaḥ sugṛhītasampradāyastālavṛntanivāsī avahitaparipālanīye karmānuṣṭhānamārge sampratipanne pramāṇe aicchikau parigrahaparityāgau cikīt | santamimamaṃśaṃ varadanāthācāryavacasā sādhveva parikalayanti vimarśakāḥ | paribhāṣā dhūrtasvāminā na vyākhyātā. dhūrtasvāmī paribhāṣāpraśne bhāṣyakṛnna veti vicārayāmaḥ daśama- praśnādyasūtrabhāṣyavṛttau 'yattvayaṃ bhāṣyakāra ' ityupakramya "paribhāṣāyāṃ vyākhyātatvācca na vyāca tasyedaṃ prayojanam - paribhāṣāprabhṛti prāyaścittaparyantaṃ yaduktamatrāpekṣitaṃ tattatra yathā vyākhyātaṃ tatheha pratipattavyam, yathā 'dīkṣaṇīyāyāstantraṃ prakramayati' ityatrā- vyākhyātastantraśabdaḥ; tantramityaṅgasamudāya iti darśapūrṇamāsayo- rvyākhyātatvāt" ityuktam | anena ca paribhāṣāyā dhūrtasvāminā vyākhyātatvaṃ pratīyate | 8 , evaṃ tañjānagarasthaprācyakośāgārasūcyāṃ dhūrtasvābhikṛtaṃ sāmānyasūtrabhāṣyaṃ nirdiśyate | bahupu paribhāṣāvyākhyānakośeṣu dhūrtasvāmibhāṣyamityeva lekho'pi dṛśyate | tathā uṣānāmnayāṃ patrikāyāṃ āpastambaparibhāṣāsūtrabhāpyaṃ dhūrtasvāmipraṇītaṃ mudrāpya prakāśitam taccādrākṣamiti paribhāṣā - sūtravyākhyāprakāśakā ma. rā mahādevaśāstriṇo vyalikhan | , tañjānagaraprācyakośasūcī prakāśakaśca svanirdiṣṭaṃ sāmānya- sūtrabhāṣyaṃ mahīśūrapuraprācyakośāgārataḥ prakāśitam | tatra cātratya- kośātkiyantaścideva varṇamātrakṛtāḥ pāṭhabhedā dṛśyanta iti itaḥ prakāśitayorvyākhyayorharadattīyavyākhyāṃ nirdiśati | XXXV haradattakṛtaparibhāṣāvyākhyā ca darśapūrṇamāsavyākhyā pratijñā- sūtrasthadhūrtasvāmibhāṣyasarūpabhūyobhāgopakramā dhūrtasvāmīyatvasambhāva- nāmupoddalayedapi | tathā'pi dhūrtasvāmī paribhāṣāpraśna bhāṣyamakaroditi cedabhyu- paima: 'yajñaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ' ityatraiva avaśyāpekṣitasya yajñavibhāgasya vyākhyāśabdārthavivaraṇasya ca kṛtatvāntra punardarśapūrṇamāsavyākhyā- pratiśāsūtre tadapekṣā bhāṣyakārādibhira vyākhyātatvaṃ vadan ādau tasyāvyākhyānaṃ spaṣṭamāha | hautrasya iṣṭiprakaraṇānte cāturmāsyānte ca dhūrtasvāminā vyākhyānāt tatra ca rāmāṇḍāreṇa vṛtte. praṇayanādane vyākhyānavacana- 1 itinidānakāramati. ' iti bhāṣyavaco'nurodhe nidānā- nantargatameva chandovicitisūtraṃ nirdiśatīti jñāyate | piṅgalanāgīyā sarvasādhāraṇī matā ityetadālocane yājuṣyapi chandovicitissaiveti niścayassyāt | yādavaprakāśaiśca yājuṣītyeva chando- vicitiḥ parigṛhītā vyākhyātā ca | piṅgala- cchandovicitau ca idaṃ vacanaṃ na paśyāmaḥ | dhūrtasvāmī ca yājuṣīmeva chandovicitiṃ nirdiśa- tītyatra ca saṃśayaḥ | tataśca kva syādida vacana miti paricintanīyam | sarvaḥ pūrvapakṣo darśasya iti nidānakāramatiḥ' iti sarvamahaḥ prātarāhute iti nidānasūtraṃ' iti ca bhāṣyam | nidānasūtraṃ nāma patañjalipraṇītaḥ chandogānā pari- karabhūteṣu dvipañcāśatsu grantheṣu kalpasūtraṃ tathā kṣudraṃ lāṭyāyanakameva ca | upagranthaḥ pañcavidho nidānaṃ tāṇḍyalakṣaṇam || anapatsthānustotraṃ kalpānupadameva ca | etaddaśavidhaṃ sūtraṃ sāmageṣu ca viśrutam || ityukteṣu sūtranibandheṣvanyatamam | lāṭyāyanamanupadaṃ nidānaṃ kalpameva ca | upagranthāśca kṣudrāśca taṇḍālakṣaṇameva ca || sūtraṃ pañcavidhīyaṃ ca 129 trirabhyastaphalīkaraṇakarma- 1 avidyamānāyā – anālambhukatvādinānimittena asānnahitāyā abhāva ca tasyā ityartho'viśeṣāt, vakṣyati ca patnayabhāve teja ādi lupyate iti | nanu 'patnīvadasyāgnihotra bhavati | yuktā me yajñamanvāsātā' ityādinā karmaṇa patnīvattvavacanāt tadabhāva kamaiva na saṃvarteta ? maivam, karma na savartetānaṅgatve, aṅgatve tu savṛtta syāt kuta ? asyāgnihotra me yajñamiti yajamānasyaiva svāmitvajñānācca na patnayā | tathā vāhiyavapaśvājyapaya kapāla- patnīsaṃbandhānīti tasyā prasiddhāṅgasamabhivyāhārācca | tathā karmacodanā api yajamānamevādhikurvanti na patnām | tasmādapatnī ko'pyagnimā harediti bahucabrāhmaṇam | patnayabhāve'pyagnihotra vadadbhi kalpāntarakārairudghāṭita caitat bharadvājajaiminyāśva- lāyanai | tathā ācāro'gyatra dṛṣṭa śiṣṭatamānā pūrveṣā kaṇvavibhaṇḍakabhīṣmādīnām bhagavataśca dāśarathe | kiñca sahādhikāre'pi patnayā yajamānasāmya nāstyeva | yathokta mīmāsakai (6-1-24), –yāvaduktamāśīrbrahmacaryamatulyatvāt' iti | tasmātsiddhamabhāve'pi patnayā na karmaṇo nivṛttiriti (ru) 2 ṇavacanādekamiti -
